# PS1 Performance Experiment Log

> 🌐 **Rendered version:** **[/docs/performance/](https://hunterdavis.com/johnny-castaway-ps1/docs/performance/)** — this doc rendered on the project website with cross-links and prose context. The GitHub copy here is the source.


Date started: 2026-04-25

Purpose: tracked, searchable ledger for PS1 scene-playback performance
experiments. Scratch JSONL logs remain useful locally, but this file records the
decision history that should survive cleanup, branch rebases, and later retry
passes.

## Experiments

| Date | ID | Commit | Hypothesis | Command | Result | Decision |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| 2026-04-25 | `fg2-window-40kb` | `4ddc89f4` | A `40 KB` FG2 stream window might reduce due misses relative to the accepted `32 KB` default without reintroducing refill overrun. | `./scripts/ps1-perf-iterate.sh --case "fishing1::fgpilot fishing1 prefetch-window 40960" --frames 4200 --timeout 180 --baseline scratch/ps1-perf-iterate/20260425-143847/summary.json --require-improvement` | Failed before `JCPERF2`; headless regtest exited `137`, gate failure `missing_jcperf2`. No usable scene metrics. Artifacts: `scratch/ps1-perf-iterate/20260425-144406/fishing1/headless-regtest.log`, `scratch/ps1-perf-iterate/20260425-144406/fishing1/perf-summary.json`. | Do not promote. Keep as a retry candidate after headless stability work or a lower-memory/dual-window design. |
| 2026-04-25 | `fg2-window-56kb` | `1fe1ceda` | A `56 KB` FG2 stream window might reduce reads and due misses enough to beat the `32 KB` default while staying below the failed `64 KB` pressure point. | `./scripts/ps1-perf-iterate.sh --case "fishing1::fgpilot fishing1 prefetch-window 57344" --frames 4200 --timeout 180 --baseline scratch/ps1-perf-iterate/20260425-143847/summary.json --require-improvement` | Completed with correct pixels/sound counters but failed the improvement gate. Baseline to current: `loop_vb 1426 -> 1442`, `blocking_vb 148 -> 170`, `prefetch_overrun_vb 104 -> 140`, `loop_reads 29 -> 16`, `due_misses 5 -> 2`. Artifacts: `scratch/ps1-perf-iterate/20260425-144631/fishing1/headless-regtest.log`, `scratch/ps1-perf-iterate/20260425-144631/fishing1/perf-summary.json`. | Do not promote. Larger windows reduce transaction count but overrun held slack more often; prefer smarter refill timing/layout over simply increasing the buffer. |
| 2026-04-25 | `fg2-no-stage1-isolation` | `816b7c28` | Disabling the one-entry stage buffer might show whether stage-copy overhead or stage/window interaction is hurting the current default. | `./scripts/ps1-perf-iterate.sh --case "fishing1::fgpilot fishing1 no-stage1" --frames 4200 --timeout 180 --baseline scratch/ps1-perf-iterate/20260425-143847/summary.json --require-improvement` | Failed before `JCPERF2`; headless regtest exited `137`, gate failure `missing_jcperf2`. No usable scene metrics. Artifacts: `scratch/ps1-perf-iterate/20260425-144914/fishing1/headless-regtest.log`, `scratch/ps1-perf-iterate/20260425-144914/fishing1/perf-summary.json`. | Do not promote and do not infer that staging is slow. Keep staging enabled until a clean isolation run says otherwise. |
| 2026-04-25 | `fg2-partial-tail-stage` | `1413e813` | When a staged frame straddles the current stream-window end, copy the resident prefix from the window and read only the missing tail into the stage buffer instead of refilling another overlapping full window. | `./scripts/ps1-perf-iterate.sh --scene fishing1 --frames 4200 --timeout 180 --baseline scratch/ps1-perf-iterate/20260425-143847/summary.json --require-improvement` | Failed. The scene emitted valid metrics but the outer headless process exited `137`; metrics also regressed. Baseline to current: `loop_vb 1426 -> 1446`, `blocking_vb 148 -> 178`, `loop_reads 29 -> 37`, `due_misses 5 -> 9`, while `partial_hits=10` and `prefetch_overrun_vb 104 -> 98`. Artifacts: `scratch/ps1-perf-iterate/20260425-145256/fishing1/headless-regtest.log`, `scratch/ps1-perf-iterate/20260425-145256/fishing1/perf-summary.json`. | Do not promote. Smaller tail reads increase CD transaction count and due misses; avoid partial-frame reads unless grouped with a broader sequential-read design. |
| 2026-04-25 | `fg2-stage-copy-fallthrough` | `93a91a6a` | If `fgRuntimeTryStageNextFrame()` only copies the next frame from an already-resident window, use the same held iteration to prefetch farther ahead instead of waiting immediately. | `./scripts/ps1-perf-iterate.sh --scene fishing1 --frames 4200 --timeout 180 --baseline scratch/ps1-perf-iterate/20260425-143847/summary.json --require-improvement` | Failed before `JCPERF2`; headless regtest exited `137`, gate failure `missing_jcperf2`. The log reached `JCPERF scene-start` and repeated FG2 directory lookups but emitted no scene-end metrics. Artifacts: `scratch/ps1-perf-iterate/20260425-145649/fishing1/headless-regtest.log`, `scratch/ps1-perf-iterate/20260425-145649/fishing1/perf-summary.json`. | Do not promote. The idea may be valid, but the naive same-iteration fallthrough appears to destabilize CD/window sequencing; revisit only with an explicit prefetch state machine. |
| 2026-04-25 | `x-aware-clean-restore` | `3cbbcae1` | Track dirty X extents per tile and use them only for RAM clean-background restore, leaving the existing full-row upload path unchanged. | `./scripts/ps1-perf-iterate.sh --scene fishing1 --frames 4200 --timeout 180 --baseline scratch/ps1-perf-iterate/20260425-143847/summary.json --allow-regression 10 --require-improvement` | Promoted. Repeat run passed with correctness clean. Baseline to current: `loop_vb 1426 -> 1366`, `overrun_vb 349 -> 289`, `blocking_vb 148 -> 125`, `restore_bytes 16035840 -> 9580428`, `due_misses 5 -> 1`; `prefetch_overrun_vb` regressed `104 -> 113` but total playback improved materially. Artifacts: `scratch/ps1-perf-iterate/20260425-150236/fishing1/headless-regtest.log`, `scratch/ps1-perf-iterate/20260425-150236/fishing1/perf-summary.json`. | Promote. This is a real render-side speedup; next pass should tackle X-aware upload so `upload_bytes` stops dominating. |
| 2026-04-25 | `fg2-pal4-opaque-span-compositor` | `8d8c7095` | FG2 PAL4 spans contain only visible pixels, so the runtime can remove the per-pixel transparent-index branch and split each span by destination tile once instead of selecting tiles per pixel. | `./scripts/ps1-perf-iterate.sh --scene fishing1 --frames 4200 --timeout 180 --baseline scratch/ps1-perf-iterate/20260425-151313/summary.json --allow-regression 2 --require-improvement` | Promoted. Two clean passes showed identical speedup; one intervening headless run hit the known pre-`JCPERF2` wrapper/container `137` failure and was rejected by the harness. Baseline to current: `loop_vb 1366 -> 1335`, `overrun_vb 289 -> 258`, `blocking_vb 125 -> 106`, `prefetch_overrun_vb 113 -> 94`. Final VRAM hash matched baseline: `330784fc30046503d5f273b28c80abe8e584d40fc7dca3ff1771e59aa57cfbb0`. Artifacts: `scratch/ps1-perf-iterate/20260425-151745/fishing1/perf-summary.json`, `scratch/ps1-perf-iterate/20260425-152021/fishing1/perf-summary.json`. | Promote. This is a compositor hot-path win with no pack format change; next candidates remain exact-X upload and smarter CD grouping. |
| 2026-04-25 | `prefetch-duplicate-probe-skip` | `7858b725` | Once the next entry is already staged, the held loop should only call window prefetch if it would issue a read; otherwise the duplicate stage/window probes are pure CPU noise. | `./scripts/ps1-perf-iterate.sh --scene fishing1 --frames 4200 --timeout 180 --baseline scratch/ps1-perf-iterate/20260425-152021/summary.json --allow-regression 2` | Promoted as a micro-cleanup. Timing stayed flat: `loop_vb 1335 -> 1335`, `blocking_vb 106 -> 106`, `prefetch_overrun_vb 94 -> 94`; duplicate no-op probes dropped `887 -> 0`. VRAM and SPU hashes matched baseline. Artifact: `scratch/ps1-perf-iterate/20260425-152635/fishing1/perf-summary.json`. | Promote. Not a visible-speed win, but it removes redundant held-loop work and makes future prefetch metrics cleaner. |
| 2026-04-25 | `prefetch-min-slack-6vb` | `17da88d1` | Reject stream-window refills unless at least `6` held VBlanks remain, so short-slack CD reads cannot overrun into visible playback. | `./scripts/ps1-perf-iterate.sh --scene fishing1 --frames 4200 --timeout 180 --baseline scratch/ps1-perf-iterate/20260425-152635/summary.json --allow-regression 2 --require-improvement` | Failed. It did reduce prefetch overrun (`94 -> 24`), but made due misses and blocking worse: `loop_vb 1335 -> 1341`, `overrun_vb 258 -> 264`, `blocking_vb 106 -> 139`, `due_misses 1 -> 14`, `skipped_no_slack 0 -> 79`. Artifact: `scratch/ps1-perf-iterate/20260425-153142/fishing1/perf-summary.json`. | Do not promote. The threshold is too strict; it proves that avoiding prefetch overrun is not enough if the due-frame path has to block more often. |
| 2026-04-25 | `prefetch-min-slack-3vb` | `17da88d1` | A smaller stream-window refill threshold should avoid the worst short-slack overrun without starving near-term frames. | `./scripts/ps1-perf-iterate.sh --scene fishing1 --frames 4200 --timeout 180 --baseline scratch/ps1-perf-iterate/20260425-152635/summary.json --allow-regression 2 --require-improvement` | Promoted. Two clean passes matched exactly. Baseline to current: `loop_vb 1335 -> 1325`, `overrun_vb 258 -> 248`, `blocking_vb 106 -> 106`, `prefetch_overrun_vb 94 -> 67`, `skipped_no_slack 0 -> 11`; due misses rose `1 -> 4` but did not increase blocking time. Artifacts: `scratch/ps1-perf-iterate/20260425-153424/fishing1/perf-summary.json`, `scratch/ps1-perf-iterate/20260425-153546/fishing1/perf-summary.json`. | Promote. This is a small but repeatable CD timing win; next CD work should reduce read cost or grouping rather than raising the threshold. |
| 2026-04-25 | `prefetch-short-slack-direct-stage` | `effbe295` | If a window refill is rejected with only `1-2` held VBlanks left, directly stage the next small entry instead of skipping all prefetch work. | `./scripts/ps1-perf-iterate.sh --scene fishing1 --frames 4200 --timeout 180 --baseline scratch/ps1-perf-iterate/20260425-153546/summary.json --allow-regression 2 --require-improvement` | Failed. One run died before `JCPERF2`; the next emitted full metrics but regressed: `loop_vb 1325 -> 1332`, `overrun_vb 248 -> 255`, `blocking_vb 106 -> 112`, `prefetch_overrun_vb 67 -> 82`, while due misses improved only `4 -> 3`. Reads rose `37 -> 42`. Artifact: `scratch/ps1-perf-iterate/20260425-154130/fishing1/perf-summary.json`. | Do not promote. More small direct reads are not free; avoid increasing CD transaction count unless pack layout or grouping also changes. |
| 2026-04-25 | `holiday-overlap-restamp` | `effbe295` | Seed holiday decoration into the clean backdrop and restamp it only when the current FG2 frame overlaps, reducing repeated holiday dirty/upload work. | `./scripts/ps1-perf-iterate.sh --scene fishing1 --frames 4200 --timeout 180 --baseline scratch/ps1-perf-iterate/20260425-153546/summary.json --allow-regression 2 --require-improvement` | Failed as a no-op. Correctness stayed clean, but all loop and gfx counters were unchanged: `loop_vb 1325 -> 1325`, `blocking_vb 106 -> 106`, `prefetch_overrun_vb 67 -> 67`, `restore_bytes 9580428 -> 9580428`, `upload_bytes 17277440 -> 17277440`. Artifact: `scratch/ps1-perf-iterate/20260425-154928/fishing1/perf-summary.json`. | Do not promote. Fishing1's active frames overlap the Christmas decoration enough that this does not reduce dirty work; revisit only for scenes/holidays where overlap is sparse. |
| 2026-04-25 | `fg2-skip-clear-otag` | `e18dfd48` | Base-diff FG2 playback uses RAM tile compositing, so skip `grBeginFrame()`/`ClearOTagR()` on rendered entries and avoid clearing an unused primitive buffer. | `./scripts/ps1-perf-iterate.sh --scene fishing1 --frames 4200 --timeout 180 --baseline scratch/ps1-perf-iterate/20260425-153546/summary.json --allow-regression 2 --require-improvement` | Failed as unmeasured. Artifact: `scratch/ps1-perf-iterate/20260425-155223/fishing1/perf-summary.json`. | Do not promote on this pass. It may save sub-VBlank CPU, but current metrics cannot prove it; retry only with a CPU-counter or instruction-budget metric. |
| 2026-04-25 | `fg2-window-24kb-post-slack` | `38d2fede` | After the `3` VBlank refill guard, a smaller `24 KB` stream window may reduce visible refill overrun enough to beat the `32 KB` default. | `./scripts/ps1-perf-iterate.sh --case "fishing1::fgpilot fishing1 prefetch-window 24576" --frames 4200 --timeout 180 --baseline scratch/ps1-perf-iterate/20260425-153546/summary.json --allow-regression 2 --require-improvement` | Promoted. One pre-metrics `137` run was inconclusive; the clean parameterized run and then the default-code run matched exactly. ## Retry Queue

| Candidate | Reason to revisit |
|---|---|
| `40 KB` stream window | Failed structurally, not because metrics proved it slow. Retry only after the headless exit-137 failure mode is understood or memory pressure is reduced elsewhere. |
| `56 KB` stream window | Correct but slower than `32 KB`; retry only if later changes can hide larger refill reads instead of blocking on them. |
| `64 KB` stream window | Earlier run also exited `137` before `JCPERF2`; likely too much pressure for the current simple single-window strategy. |
| `16 KB` stream window | Produced partial metrics but regtest exited `137`; metrics were worse on `blocking_vb` and `due_misses`, so this is low priority. |
| `no-stage1` window-only path | Failed structurally before metrics; retry only after headless stability improves or after stage/window code is simplified. |
| Partial tail reads | Proved harmful in isolation because fewer bytes did not compensate for more transactions; retry only as part of pack/group layout work. |
| Same-iteration stage-copy fallthrough | Structural failure before metrics; retry only with explicit state that prevents repeated FG2 lookup/read churn. |
| `6` VBlank prefetch slack threshold | Correct but slower; retry only if later pack/layout changes reduce due-frame miss cost enough that stricter prefetch gating can win. |
| Short-slack direct staging | Correctness clean but slower; retry only if direct-read transaction overhead is reduced by grouped or physically adjacent pack reads. |
| Holiday overlap restamping | Correctness clean but no measured fishing1 benefit; retry only after per-scene/holiday overlap stats show sparse contact. |
| Skip FG2 `ClearOTagR` | Correctness clean but no VBlank-level movement; retry after adding a cheap CPU-work metric or if primitive-buffer cleanup becomes a code-size pass. |
| X-aware upload via one scratch rectangle | Booted but failed to finish before `JCPERF2`; retry only with 16-pixel-aligned strip batching and no extra per-partial `DrawSync` calls. |
| `22 KB`, `23 KB`, `26 KB`, and `28 KB` post-slack windows | All lost to the accepted smaller-window point or tied without improvement; retry only after pack grouping or async refill changes the CD cost model. |
| `1` VBlank post-20 KB refill guard | Correctness clean but slower; retry only if later CD grouping or async refill makes the short-slack read cost materially cheaper. |
| `4` VBlank staged-copy fallthrough guard after the post-merge baseline | Correctness clean but identical to the accepted `5` VBlank guard; retry only after another change alters staged-copy/window timing. |
| `4` VBlank staged-copy fallthrough guard after `16 KB`/`3` VBlank retune | Correctness clean and lower blocking, but total `loop_vb` regressed by `10`; retry only after refill/grouping changes make the extra fallthrough read cheaper. |
| Split immediate/lookahead refill guards | Correctness clean and recovered due misses, but total `loop_vb` regressed by `14`; retry only after grouped or sector-aligned refills reduce short-read cost. |
| Screenwide PAL4 row dirty marking | Correctness clean and faster, but widened restore across the tile boundary; retry only as per-tile row aggregation or pack-emitted per-tile extents. |
| `4` VBlank refill guard after per-tile row dirty marking | Correctness clean and slightly faster overall, but visible blocking regressed; retry only if grouped/pipelined coverage prevents due misses. |
| `15 KB` and `14 KB` windows after the `6` VBlank fallthrough guard | `15 KB` is a no-op under rounding; `14 KB` lowers overrun but starves due frames. Retry only after pack grouping makes smaller windows cover more useful entries. |
| PAL4 visible-row dirty-branch hoist | Correctness clean but no VBlank-level movement; retry only with finer CPU counters or if grouped with other compositor branch cleanup. |
| Direct `markTileDirtyRect()` from PAL4 row dirty aggregation | Correctness clean but slower than the generic wrapper in this build; retry only if dirty marking is refactored into a smaller shared inline path. |
| FG2 compose-before-VSync sequencing | Correctness clean but slower and worse for CD blocking; retry only with Detail-tier render counters or after separating render scheduling from prefetch cadence. |
| `7` VBlank staged-copy fallthrough guard after per-tile row dirty marking | Correctness clean but slower with no blocking improvement; retry only after the refill-cost knee moves. |
| Staged next-VBlank present scheduler | Correctness and visual-work identity stayed clean, but total loop time regressed by `71` VBlanks; retry only after render preparation and CD prefetch have separate slack budgets. |
| Prepared-present thresholds `2`, `3`, and `5` | `2` VBlanks reduced loop time but failed the strict blocking/refill gate; `3` VBlanks kept loop flat while regressing blocking/refill and adding prep work; `5` VBlanks added prep work without timing improvement. Retry only if prepared-frame selection becomes exact-use instead of extra speculative restore/compose work. |
| Prepared-stage decoupling | Correctness-clean only after blocking CD reads during prepared waits, which made fishing1 flat at `loop_vb=1221`; retry only with explicit prepared-present/CD-budget metrics or a real second visual buffer. |
| Exact-4 VBlank prepared-present gating alone | Earlier pre-pause probes reduced speculative restore/compose calls but regressed loop, blocking, and refill-overrun by one VBlank each. The post-pause combined exact-4 plus 4-row upload-gap checkpoint is accepted only as a flat-key-timing work-reduction pass. |
| 4-or-5 VBlank prepared-present gating | Regressed loop/blocking/refill and increased prep calls; do not retry max-slack caps without prepared-used counters. |
| Prepared-wait stream-window prefetch | Post-gap1 retry is now accepted as flat-timing work reduction (`restore_calls/compose_calls 166 -> 155`). Further retries should be part of a full prepared/CD scheduler, not the old opportunistic shape. |
| Prepared-present `>=4` threshold after prepared-wait prefetch | Exact timing and work no-op with only loop bookkeeping movement; keep exact-`4` unless a new scheduler signal changes the budget. |
| `5` VBlank staged-copy fallthrough after prepared-wait prefetch | Still exposes extra CD pressure; keep the accepted `6` VBlank guard unless grouped reads make the same-iteration lookahead cheaper. |
| Long-hold catch-up threshold `4` after prepared-wait prefetch | Still regresses target and visible CD pressure; keep threshold `5` until grouped/predictive prefetch changes the slack budget. |
| Prefetch would-read probe removal | Regressed by shifting CD phase (`blocking_vb 5 -> 6`); retry only as part of an explicit pacing/scheduler rewrite. |
| Prefetch candidate reuse | Kept exact cadence flat but grew the ELF and did not move any tracked work counter; retry only as part of a broader prefetch state-machine rewrite or with finer CPU counters. |
| Host-timing preconvert on load | Runtime-flat but code-growth/unstable; retry only if finer CPU counters prove `fgEntryHoldVBlanks()` is material. |
| PAL4 aligned pair store | Dynamic alignment branch regressed CD phase badly; retry only as generated/assembly compositor work without runtime branching. |
| Caption render default-off gate | Runtime-flat but grew the executable; retry only with an inline/global caption-active flag rather than another function call. |
| Automatic FGHEAP probe compile gate | Failed as a standalone cleanup on the old fishing1 phase, but is now accepted as layout recovery for the FISHING3 high segmented setup-prime probe. Keep it tied to an otherwise-gated speed/layout win; do not treat heap-probe removal alone as a VBlank optimization. |
| Prepared-current RAM background reuse | Removed all duplicate prep work but regressed the same one-VBlank timing shape; retry only with explicit replacement pacing. |
| Post-prefetch leftover prepare | More prep work after CD reads created five due misses and many more backward seeks; retry only with frame-level budgeting that proves future coverage is preserved. |

| `5` VBlank fallthrough guard after prepared-present | Timing flat but no key improvement, and hidden CD read time worsened; keep `6` VBlanks until grouped reads or prepared-used counters change the slack model. |
| Direct-stage payload cap tuning | Post-gap8 and post-caption sweeps confirm `8 KB` as the fishing1 knee: `6 KB`, `7 KB`, and `10 KB` are no-ops, while `4 KB` regresses blocking/refill. Retry only after grouped reads or a second stage slot changes direct-stage coverage cost. |
| Split immediate/lookahead refill guards | Raising pure-lookahead refills to `4` VBlanks regressed loop/blocking/refill while preserving due misses; fixed scalar guard splitting is exhausted until read-cost prediction or pack groups exist. |

| Clean-rect restore intersection skip | Correctness stayed clean but CD/prefetch metrics regressed; retry only if clean-rect grouping changes enough that skipped restore scans are common and the check can be made pack-time or table-driven. |
| CD stream file-start LBA cache | Correctness clean but exact VBlank-level no-op; retry only with finer CPU counters or during a broader CD helper cleanup pass. |

| Direct-stage scratch merge into stream window | Correctness clean but regressed loop/blocking/refill with extra prep calls; retry only after read histograms or pack groups show a specific overlap case worth preserving. |
| Proactive stream-window extension while next payload is resident | Preserved zero due misses but doubled visible CD pressure; retry only when read-cost/group metadata can distinguish cheap append opportunities from slack-stealing reads. |
| Inline PAL4 span compositor | Correctness and work identity were stable but total loop, blocking, and refill overrun regressed; retry only as generated scene-specialized code, not as a local helper-inline rewrite. |
| Slack-adaptive `8/12/16 KB` stream windows | Lowered refill overrun and total loop slightly but regressed visible blocking; retry only after pack grouping preserves due-frame coverage. |

| `14/18/20 KB` stream windows after prepared-present | All regressed total loop versus the accepted `16 KB` default; retry window sizes only after grouped/pack-aware reads change useful coverage per transaction. |
| Leading empty setup consume | Saves `6` loop VBlanks by skipping a non-payload startup render, but raises blocking/refill overrun by one VBlank and changes render count; retry only with CD smoothing and visual signoff policy for empty capture artifacts. |
| Zero-delay `eventsWaitTick` fast path | Exact VBlank-level no-op; retry only with finer CPU counters or during event/pause cleanup. |
| `19 KB` requested window after the post-2VB sweep | Sector-rounds to the old `20 KB` default and is a no-op; use sector-sized buckets for future sweeps. |
| `125 Hz` / `65 Hz` SPI pad polling | Correctness clean but `loop_vb` regressed by one VBlank while only CD submetrics improved; retry only with finer CPU/IRQ counters or a pause-input validation harness. |
| Post-row-restore `16/20/24/32 KB` window sweep under the `2` VBlank guard | Correctness clean but all tested sizes regressed total loop versus the then-accepted `18 KB` default; the `16 KB` point became useful only when paired with the later `3` VBlank guard. |
| Targeted dirty-row state clearing | Correctness clean but no VBlank-level improvement; retry only with finer CPU counters or when dirty-state maintenance is being refactored anyway. |
| Targeted current dirty-row clearing | Exact key timing no-op with a small prepared-work identity shift; retry only if lower-level CPU counters can prove the row-clear cost matters. |
| Runtime PAL4 pair LUT and aligned word stores | Correctness clean but slower than direct halfword palette writes; retry as pack-time direct16 or with lower-overhead scene-palette specialization. |
| 16-pixel X-aware upload strips with per-strip sync | Failed to reach scene-end within the headless frame window; retry only with a bounded scratch arena that allows batching partial strips behind one final `DrawSync`. |
| 32-pixel X-aware upload arena | Correctness clean and byte volume improved, but rect count exploded to `11575`; retry only with deterministic rect-count caps or wide-band merging. |
| Runtime scratch-packed X upload | Even capped tile-band packing regressed because CPU copy cost dominated; retry only if the pack/runtime stores upload-ready contiguous bands or the GPU upload source becomes naturally contiguous. |
| FG2 sound-event table in metadata prefix | Setup reads improved, but moving the table ahead of payload shifted every payload by `36` bytes and badly worsened active CD phase; retry only with payload offsets/sector crossings preserved or with pack-group metadata that makes the new layout intentional. |
| FG2 resolve-once startup reads | Setup improved, but active playback regressed and speculative prep cadence changed; retry only after adding an explicit setup/loop cadence barrier or decoupling prepared-present from startup timing. |
| Trim stream-window reads to complete payloads | Reduced bytes/sectors while preserving hits and due misses, but increased elapsed loop CD time and visible blocking; retry only with per-read cost classes or emitted group metadata. |
| Prepare staged frame before lookahead prefetch | Reduced restore/compose work but regressed CD blocking and seek-backs; retry only with separate render-prep and prefetch slack budgets that prove future read coverage is preserved. |
| Prefetch during prepared-frame waits | Removed duplicate prepared work but regressed blocking/refill by one VBlank; retry only after group metadata or a scheduler cost model proves the read fits the remaining held cadence. |
| Prepared-present min slack 5 | Saved only three prep calls and regressed blocking/refill by two VBlanks; do not retry threshold-only prepared-present pruning without a new scheduler signal. |
| Pad diagnostics compile gate | Large binary-size win but active timing regressed badly; retry only with a layout-preserving/cold-section strategy or after the CD scheduler no longer depends on current code shape. |
| Visual debug screen compile gate with EXE layout pad | Preserving `FISHING1.FG2` at LBA `390` did not recover the earlier 4 KB code-size win; retry only after scheduler phase is explicitly controlled or cold debug code can be isolated without changing hot code placement. |
| Dirty-row clear via `memset` | Smaller code and a simpler source shape still regressed active cadence, including with the EXE padded back to the accepted LBA; retry only with a custom inline MIPS fill or a packed dirty-state layout that changes the operation count, not just the library call. |
| Setup-settle first-frame prerender | It improved active `loop_vb` by moving first-frame render work into setup, but full `scene_vb` stayed flat and visible CD pressure regressed; retry only if the scheduler can preserve CD phase or if the setup render actually lowers full-scene timing. |
| Compose-ever telemetry removal | Exact timing no-op and loadable text grew by `28` bytes despite removing a hot store; retry only as part of a broader foreground telemetry/API pruning pass with map/layout review. |
| Long-hold catch-up threshold `4` | No loop win and worse CD pressure; keep the accepted threshold `5` until grouped prefetch or a better slack budget can prove additional catch-up is free. |
| Prepared-present threshold `5` after catch-up | Reduced duplicate prep and nominal loop by one VBlank, but regressed visible CD pressure; retry only with grouped/read-cost-aware prefetch. |
| Long-hold extra catch-up for `>=9` VBlank holds | Exact no-op under fishing1; retry only if another scene's hold distribution shows actual late long-hold opportunities. |
| `4 KB` FG2 metadata prefix | Reduced setup bytes and one setup read VBlank but regressed active CD pressure; retry only after setup-to-loop phase is deliberately normalized or group metadata decouples playback cadence from startup read shape. |
| Lookahead-covered short-hold catch-up | Immediate next-frame staging plus following-window coverage still regressed visible CD pressure; retry only with an explicit render-prep/CD budget, not another timing guard. |
| Cached no-holiday compose gate | Exact timing no-op and larger ELF; retry only through generated per-variant compose code or broader overlay cleanup, not another runtime flag. |
| Dirty-row pointer swap | Saved one total loop VBlank and shrank the binary, but regressed visible CD/refill by three VBlanks; retry once CD scheduling can tolerate the changed render/prep phase. |
| Clean-rect dirty-Y narrowing | VBlank-level no-op that increased ELF size; retry only with finer CPU counters or when pack-emitted restore bands replace runtime row scans. |
| Adaptive high-slack `24 KB` stream window | Fewer reads and lower hidden loop read time still regressed visible blocking/refill; retry only when pack groups or a read-cost predictor can prove the larger read fits the held cadence. |
| High-slack `24 KB` capacity with `10+` VBlank guard | Even the conservative long-hold-only form kept `loop_vb` flat and regressed visible pressure; retry only with exact group boundaries plus append/preserve cost, not a raw held-slack threshold. |
| Adaptive high-slack `20 KB` stream window | Smaller than the `24 KB` probe but still regressed visible blocking/refill; do not retry raw extended windows without pack/read-cost metadata. |
| Prepared-buffer release | Removed duplicate prepared restore/compose work but either regressed visible CD or starved the due-frame path; retry only as a full scheduler redesign with explicit prepared-used and payload-buffer ownership states. |
| `3` VBlank refill guard after row-level restore | Correctness clean and faster in total loop, but blocking and due misses regressed too far; retry only after grouped/pipelined prefetch coverage prevents starvation. |
| `4` VBlank refill guard after setup-prime | Correctness clean and faster in total loop, but due misses returned and `blocking_vb` regressed from `13` to `19-20`; retry only after grouped or physically adjacent reads preserve zero due misses under the stricter guard. |
| `18 KB` stream window after setup-prime | Due misses stayed zero, but `loop_vb`, `blocking_vb`, and `prefetch_overrun_vb` all regressed; keep the sector-rounded `16 KB` default until grouped/pipelined reads change the CD cost model. |
| Cached base-diff runtime flag | Exact VBlank-level no-op; retry only with finer CPU counters or when doing broader runtime-state/code-size cleanup. |
| Single-row exact-width upload | Fishing1 never hit the single-row upload case under current dirty aggregation; retry only if another scene's dirty distribution shows one-row bands. |
| Narrow upload bookkeeping locals | ELF shrank but timing regressed sharply; retry only with map/register review or upload-path translation-unit isolation. |
| Staged-frame prep at `3` VBlanks | Improved nominal overrun but regressed visible CD pressure; retry only with explicit render-prep/CD slack budgeting. |
| Direct-stage read into stream window | `8 KB` aligned windows made the loop two VBlanks faster but added one visible CD VBlank; `6 KB` was a no-op. Retry only with grouped/predicted direct-stage windows. |
| `5` VBlank fallthrough guard after OT-clear skip | Regressed total loop and refill overrun; keep `6` VBlanks unless another CD-cost change moves the knee. |
| Async stream-window refill | Naive async polling regressed visible blocking badly; retry only after adding first-class async counters and CD-state ownership, not as an inline replacement for synchronous refill. |
| Prepared-present min slack 3 | Increased speculative prep and still regressed visible CD/refill by four VBlanks; do not retry threshold-only prepared-present tuning until a scheduler model can prove whether prep ballast or CD work should own each held slice. |
| Stream-window grow capacity | A `24 KB` retained window with `16 KB` normal reads improved total loop by one VBlank but reintroduced due misses and raised `blocking_vb` to `16`; retry only when pack-emitted read groups or per-read cost classes can cap transaction churn. |
| FG2 fixed 16 KB payload grouping | Lowered read count but increased sectors and visible blocking; retry only as selective group metadata that pads or groups around measured hot boundaries, not every arbitrary `16 KB` crossing. |
| FG2 payload-start sector alignment | A cheap `904`-byte global offset shift doubled visible blocking; do not globally align payload bases without preserving measured active-loop offset cadence. |
| FG2 pause-poll-only events | Reduced apparent work but regressed loop/blocking/refill by one VBlank; retry only with a full FG2-owned event/present scheduler, not a local event-tail removal. |
| Post-pause raw stream-window sizes `18 KB` and `14 KB` | Both lost against the exact no-holiday post-pause `16 KB` default: `18 KB` overran held slack, and `14 KB` introduced `12` due misses. Retry raw window sizes only after pack groups or a costed read scheduler change useful coverage per transaction. |
| `cdrom_ps1.c -O3` | Whole-TU optimization grew the executable by three sectors, shifted `FISHING1.FG2` three LBAs later, and raised visible CD/refill pressure from `5` to `13` VBlanks. Do not retry hot whole-TU `-O3` until code layout and CD phase can be held fixed. |
| Pure FG2 LBA shift | Adding `+1`, `+2`, `+3`, `+4`, or `+8` dummy sectors after the executable moved `FISHING1.FG2` as far as LBA `407` with exact flat timing. Do not spend more time on pack LBA alone; executable/code/startup phase is the remaining phase-control target. |
| Held-loop prefetch pre-check removal | Shrank code and removed a redundant lookup, but with pack LBA restored it still regressed visible CD pressure. Treat hot-loop source cleanups as phase-sensitive until hot-symbol padding or a scheduler barrier exists. |
| Pure executable padding | Adding `2 KB` of initialized executable payload shifted hot symbols by `+8` and kept timing flat. Padding is a control tool, not a speed win by itself. |
| `fgRuntimeFillWindowForEntry` alignment | `+4`, `+20`, and downstream `+36` byte hot-symbol shifts were timing-flat. Do not expect small positive alignment shifts alone to close the current gap. |
| Upload rect cap `6` | Re-tested cleanly but still provides no measured binary/timing win and removes cross-scene headroom. Keep cap `8` until matrix data says otherwise. |
| WALKSTUF3 low padded FGP3 | Payload only shrank `17.8%`, loop improved by `9` VBlanks, and `prefetch_overrun_vb` regressed by `4`. Retry only with generated grouping/direct16/residual metadata that changes read shape, not plain residual conversion. |
| Prepared-present event-wait removal | Regressed visible CD pressure and weakens input polling; keep the explicit poll until a broader present/input scheduler replaces it. |
| Owned-idle 4 VBlank catch-up | The new ownership counters showed no eligible idle catch-up slots in fishing1 (`catchup_idle=0`) and the prototype moved the executable/CD layout. Retry only after per-frame ownership analysis proves a real slot exists. |
| Deep trace pipeline counters | Done behind `PS1_PERF_DEEP_TRACE=ON`; do not compile into the accepted speed binary because the ungated version changed EXE size/layout and timing. |
| Prepared bridge slack `>=4` | Do not retry as a local threshold. It moved ownership buckets but did not increase useful prepared-present work or improve `loop_vb`. |
| Global catch-up threshold `4` | Do not retry globally; it regressed visible CD pressure to `16` VBlanks. Threshold `4` is only safe when setup-prime or future scheduler metadata proves coverage. |
| Setup-prime `192 KB` or `256 KB` windows | Do not retry blind sizes. `256 KB` was close but still raised visible CD pressure, and `192 KB` lost; future sizes need generated per-scene/tide coverage analysis. |
| Setup-prime `320 KB` window | Done for fishing1 high tide as an active-loop win with setup-cost trade. Extend only through explicit inter-scene preload or generated setup-prime policy, not by assuming setup time is free. |

## Harness Notes

- `regtest_exit=137` means the waited process was killed by `SIGKILL`, but it is not always a PS1/emulator failure. If `JCPERF2` is complete, the case gate is clean, and DuckStation logs `Exiting with success`, the perf harness treats it as a post-success wrapper failure and continues to aggregate the run.
- `regtest_exit=137` with missing `JCPERF2` remains a hard failure. Those runs are still recorded as structural failures because they do not prove correctness or speed.
- As of the harness log fix after the dirty-row `memset` miss, `scratch/ps1-perf-iterate/experiments.jsonl` is appended after baseline comparison, so `gate_pass` includes `--baseline` and `--require-improvement` failures. Older JSONL rows were regtest/case-gate only; use their run `summary.json` for final baseline-gate status.

## Promotion Rule

An experiment is promotable when the headless perf gate passes, correctness
tripwires remain zero, and either at least one key speed metric improves without
a material regression in `loop_vb`, `blocking_vb`, `prefetch_overrun_vb`, or
scene identity, or all key timing metrics stay flat while a material
work-volume/code-size metric improves. Work-reduction promotions must be labeled
as such and must not be counted as VBlank speed wins. Failed experiments still
get recorded here so later changes can make them eligible for retry.
